AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text="## Root cause\n\n`DocumentEditFragment.onResume()` unconditionally re-assigned the editor's text:\n\n```java\npublic void onResume() {\n    super.onResume();\n    checkReloadDisk();\n    _hlEditor.setText(_document.getContent());   // <-- resets selection to 0\n}\n```\n\n`TextView.setText()` discards the current selection and places the cursor at offset 0. Since `onResume()` runs every time the app comes back to the foreground (app switch, or returning from the file-picker used by the insert-image/insert-link dialog), the cursor was silently thrown back to the start of the document. That also explains the second symptom: the insert-image dialog inserts at `_hlEditor.getSelectionStart()` when OK is pressed, so after an app switch the image landed at the beginning of the file.\n\nThe `setText` call was also redundant — `checkReloadDisk()` already reloads the document (via `loadDocumentIntoUi()`) when the file changed on disk, and `onPause()` keeps `_document` in sync with the editor content.\n\n## Fix (`DocumentEditFragment.java`)\n\n1. **Removed the unconditional `setText()` from `onResume()`** — the actual defect. Content refresh is left to `checkReloadDisk()`, which only reloads when the file really changed.\n2. **`loadDocumentIntoUi()` now only calls `setText()` when the content actually differs** from what's in the editor, so the reload path no longer needlessly drops cursor/scroll position; the cursor is then restored through a new helper.\n3. **Added `setEditorCursorPosition(int)`** which clamps the position into `[0, length]`. This also fixes an off-by-one in the old code (`_hlEditor.length() - 1`, which placed the cursor one character before the end, and `-1` on an empty document).\n4. **Persist/restore the cursor position via `SAVESTATE_CURSOR_POS`** in `onSaveInstanceState()`/`onViewCreated()`, so the position also survives view re-creation (rotation, low-memory process restart).\n\n## Verification\n\n`./gradlew clean assembleDebug` cannot run in this environment: the build script pins Android Gradle Plugin 3.0.1 from jcenter/google, neither of which is cached locally, and network access is closed — the build fails at buildscript dependency resolution before any compilation, identically on the unmodified tree.\n\nTo confirm the change compiles, I type-checked the sources directly with `javac` against `android-27/android.jar`, the cached support-library/ButterKnife 8.8.1/lifecycle artifacts, and generated `R`/`BuildConfig` plus stubs for the non-cached third-party libs. `DocumentEditFragment.java` and all of its transitive project dependencies compile with **zero errors**, and a per-file pass over the whole `app/src/main/java` tree reports no errors either.")], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 120, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 66839, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 120, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 1, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfYQVdLjQZkzW4vGdxabh', stop_reason=None, session_id='fdc925bf-bb54-4bb1-a9aa-4aba53806ef0', uuid='22e255a2-5cb8-471c-b700-a1f79ca0765c')
